</head>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/sweetalert2@11"></script>

<body>
  <header>
    <h1>Painel do Operador</h1>
    <div class="header-actions">
      <span id="ws-status">
        <span class="status-indicator disconnected" id="status-dot"></span>
        <span id="status-text">Desconectado</span>
      </span>
      <a href="/static/dashboard.html">Dashboard</a>
      <a href="/static/painel_dono.html">Painel Admin</a>
      <button onclick="logout()">Sair</button>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main>
    <section>
      <h2>Conversas Ativas</h2>
      <div class="filter-section">
        <div class="filter-controls">
          <label for="conversation-filter">Filtro:</label>
          <select id="conversation-filter" class="filter-select" onchange="filterConversations()">
            <option value="all">Todas</option>
            <option value="active">Ativas</option>
            <option value="pending">Pendentes</option>
          </select>
          <button onclick="refreshConversations()" style="padding: 0.5rem;">Atualizar</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="all-conversations">
        <p style="color: #999; text-align: center;">Carregando conversas...</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section>
      <h2>Minhas Conversas</h2>
      <div id="my-conversations">
        <p style="color: #999; text-align: center;">Nenhuma conversa atribuida</p>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section>
      <h2>Chat</h2>
      <div id="chat">
        <div id="chat-messages">
          <div class="no-chat-selected">
            <h3>Selecione uma conversa</h3>
            <p>Clique em uma conversa na lista para visualizar as mensagens</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="chat-input">
          <input type="text" id="message-input" placeholder="Digite sua mensagem..." disabled>
          <button id="send-button" onclick="sendMessage()" disabled>Enviar</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>

  <!-- Botao de Pedidos -->
  <button class="orders-toggle" onclick="toggleOrders()">
    Pedidos <span class="badge" id="orders-count">0</span>
  </button>

  <!-- Painel de Pedidos -->
  <div class="orders-panel" id="orders-panel">
    <div class="orders-header">
      Pedidos Pendentes
      <button class="close-btn" onclick="toggleOrders()">&times;</button>
    </div>
    <div class="orders-list" id="orders-list">
      <p style="color: #999; text-align: center;">Carregando...</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // Configuracao
    const API_BASE = '';
    let token = localStorage.getItem("token") || localStorage.getItem("access_token");
    let currentChatPhone = null;
    let ws = null;

    // Verificar autenticacao
    if (!token) {
      window.location.href = "/static/index.html";
    }

    // WebSocket
    function connectWebSocket() {
      const wsProtocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
      ws = new WebSocket(`${wsProtocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/dashboard`);

      ws.onopen = () => {
        console.log('WebSocket conectado');
        updateConnectionStatus(true);
      };

      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
        handleWebSocketMessage(data);
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        console.log('WebSocket desconectado');
        updateConnectionStatus(false);
        // Reconectar apos 3 segundos
        setTimeout(connectWebSocket, 3000);
      };

      ws.onerror = (error) => {
        console.error('WebSocket erro:', error);
        updateConnectionStatus(false);
      };
    }

    function updateConnectionStatus(connected) {
      const dot = document.getElementById('status-dot');
      const text = document.getElementById('status-text');

      if (connected) {
        dot.className = 'status-indicator connected';
        text.textContent = 'Conectado';
      } else {
        dot.className = 'status-indicator disconnected';
        text.textContent = 'Desconectado';
      }
    }

    function handleWebSocketMessage(data) {
      console.log('WebSocket message:', data);

      if (data.type === 'new_message') {
        // Nova mensagem recebida
        if (currentChatPhone === data.data.phone) {
          addMessageToChat(data.data);
        }
        refreshConversations();
      }

      if (data.type === 'new_order') {
        showOrderNotification(data.data);
        loadOrders();
      }

      if (data.type === 'order_update') {
        loadOrders();
      }
    }

    // Funcoes de API
    async function fetchAPI(endpoint, options = {}) {
      const defaultOptions = {
        headers: {
          'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
        }
      };

      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
        ...defaultOptions,
        ...options,
        headers: { ...defaultOptions.headers, ...options.headers }
      });

      if (response.status === 401) {
        localStorage.removeItem('token');
        localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
        window.location.href = '/static/index.html';
        return null;
      }

      return response;
    }

    // Carregar conversas
    async function loadConversations() {
      try {
        const response = await fetchAPI('/api/chats');
        if (!response || !response.ok) return;

        const chats = await response.json();
        renderConversations(chats);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar conversas:', error);
      }
    }

    function renderConversations(chats) {
      const container = document.getElementById('all-conversations');

      if (!chats || chats.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center;">Nenhuma conversa ativa</p>';
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = chats.map(chat => `
        <div class="conversation ${currentChatPhone === chat.phone ? 'active' : ''}"
             data-phone="${chat.phone}"
             onclick="selectChat('${chat.phone}')">
          <p><strong>${chat.customer_name || 'Cliente'}</strong></p>
          <p><small>${formatPhone(chat.phone)}</small></p>
          <p style="color: #666; font-size: 12px;">${chat.last_message ? chat.last_message.substring(0, 50) + '...' : 'Sem mensagens'}</p>
          <small>Estado: ${chat.state || 'inicio'}</small>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function refreshConversations() {
      loadConversations();
    }

    function filterConversations() {
      const filter = document.getElementById('conversation-filter').value;
      loadConversations();
    }

    // Selecionar chat
    async function selectChat(phone) {
      currentChatPhone = phone;

      // Atualizar visual
      document.querySelectorAll('.conversation').forEach(el => {
        el.classList.toggle('active', el.dataset.phone === phone);
      });

      // Habilitar input
      document.getElementById('message-input').disabled = false;
      document.getElementById('send-button').disabled = false;

      // Carregar mensagens
      await loadMessages(phone);
    }

    async function loadMessages(phone) {
      try {
        const response = await fetchAPI(`/api/chats/${phone}/messages`);
        if (!response || !response.ok) return;

        const messages = await response.json();
        renderMessages(messages);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar mensagens:', error);
      }
    }

    function renderMessages(messages) {
      const container = document.getElementById('chat-messages');

      if (!messages || messages.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="no-chat-selected">
            <h3>Sem mensagens</h3>
            <p>Esta conversa ainda nao tem mensagens</p>
          </div>
        `;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = messages.map(msg => {
        const msgClass = msg.from_me ? 'agent' : 'customer';
        const senderName = msg.from_me ? 'Atendente' : 'Cliente';
        const timeStr = new Date(msg.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit'
        });

        return `
          <div class="message ${msgClass}">
            <div class="message-content-wrapper">
              <div class="message-header">
                <span class="sender-name">${senderName}</span>
              </div>
              <div class="message-text">${escapeHtml(msg.text)}</div>
              <div class="message-footer">
                <span class="message-timestamp">${timeStr}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');

      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
    }

    function addMessageToChat(data) {
      const container = document.getElementById('chat-messages');
      const noChat = container.querySelector('.no-chat-selected');
      if (noChat) noChat.remove();

      const msgClass = data.direction === 'outgoing' ? 'agent' : 'customer';
      const senderName = data.direction === 'outgoing' ? 'Atendente' : 'Cliente';
      const timeStr = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit'
      });

      const messageDiv = document.createElement('div');
      messageDiv.className = `message ${msgClass}`;
      messageDiv.innerHTML = `
        <div class="message-content-wrapper">
          <div class="message-header">
            <span class="sender-name">${senderName}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="message-text">${escapeHtml(data.message)}</div>
          <div class="message-footer">
            <span class="message-timestamp">${timeStr}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      container.appendChild(messageDiv);
      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
    }

    // Enviar mensagem
    async function sendMessage() {
      const input = document.getElementById('message-input');
      const message = input.value.trim();

      if (!message || !currentChatPhone) return;

      input.disabled = true;
      document.getElementById('send-button').disabled = true;

      try {
        const response = await fetchAPI(`/api/chats/${currentChatPhone}/send`, {
          method: 'POST',
          body: JSON.stringify({ phone: currentChatPhone, message })
        });

        if (response && response.ok) {
          // Adicionar mensagem ao chat
          addMessageToChat({
            message,
            direction: 'outgoing'
          });
          input.value = '';
        } else {
          showError('Erro ao enviar mensagem');
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao enviar:', error);
        showError('Erro ao enviar mensagem');
      } finally {
        input.disabled = false;
        document.getElementById('send-button').disabled = false;
        input.focus();
      }
    }

    // Pedidos
    function toggleOrders() {
      const panel = document.getElementById('orders-panel');
      panel.classList.toggle('show');
      if (panel.classList.contains('show')) {
        loadOrders();
      }
    }

    async function loadOrders() {
      try {
        const response = await fetchAPI('/api/orders?status=pending');
        if (!response || !response.ok) return;

        const orders = await response.json();
        renderOrders(orders);
        updateOrdersCount(orders.length);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar pedidos:', error);
      }
    }

    function renderOrders(orders) {
      const container = document.getElementById('orders-list');

      if (!orders || orders.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Nenhum pedido pendente</p>';
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = orders.map(order => `
        <div class="order-card-mini ${order.status}">
          <h4>
            Pedido #${order.order_number || order.id}
            <span class="order-badge">${order.status.toUpperCase()}</span>
          </h4>
          <p><strong>${order.delivery_bairro || 'N/A'}</strong></p>
          <p style="color: #667eea; font-weight: bold;">R$ ${parseFloat(order.total_amount || 0).toFixed(2)}</p>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function updateOrdersCount(count) {
      const badge = document.getElementById('orders-count');
      const toggle = document.querySelector('.orders-toggle');
      badge.textContent = count;

      if (count > 0) {
        toggle.classList.add('has-orders');
      } else {
        toggle.classList.remove('has-orders');
      }
    }

    function showOrderNotification(order) {
      // Notificacao do navegador
      if (Notification.permission === 'granted') {
        new Notification('Novo Pedido!', {
          body: `#${order.order_number || order.id} - R$ ${parseFloat(order.total_amount || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
        });
      }

      // Toast
      showToast(`Novo pedido #${order.order_number || order.id}`, 'info');
    }

    // Utilidades
    function formatPhone(phone) {
      if (!phone) return '';
      const cleaned = phone.replace(/\D/g, '');
      if (cleaned.length === 13) {
        return `+${cleaned.slice(0,2)} (${cleaned.slice(2,4)}) ${cleaned.slice(4,9)}-${cleaned.slice(9)}`;
      }
      return phone;
    }

    function escapeHtml(text) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.textContent = text;
      return div.innerHTML;
    }

    function logout() {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
      window.location.href = '/static/index.html';
    }

    function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
      Swal.fire({
        toast: true,
        position: 'top-end',
        icon: type,
        title: message,
        showConfirmButton: false,
        timer: 3000,
        timerProgressBar: true
      });
    }

    function showError(message) {
      Swal.fire({
        icon: 'error',
        title: 'Erro',
        text: message
      });
    }

    // Event listeners
    document.getElementById('message-input').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        sendMessage();
      }
    });

    // Inicializacao
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      // Pedir permissao para notificacoes
      if (Notification.permission === 'default') {
        Notification.requestPermission();
      }

      connectWebSocket();
      loadConversations();
      loadOrders();

      // Atualizar periodicamente
      setInterval(loadConversations, 30000);
      setInterval(loadOrders, 30000);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    let token = localStorage.getItem("access_token");
    let currentConversationId = null;
    let ws = null;
    //const timeStr = formatDate(m.timestamp);

    if (!token) {
      showError("Token ausente. FaÃ§a login novamente.");
      window.location.href = "index.html";
    }

    // Conecta ao WebSocket com o token
    function connectWebSocket() {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("access_token");
      if (!token) return;

      ws = new WebSocket(`ws://${window.location.host}/ws?token=${token}`);

      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
        console.log("WebSocket message received:", JSON.stringify(data, null, 2));

        // Novo pedido - mostrar notificaÃ§Ã£o
        if (data.type === 'new_order') {
          showOrderNotification(data.order);
          loadOrders();
          return;
        }

        // AtualizaÃ§Ã£o de pedido
        if (data.type === 'order_update') {
          console.log('Pedido atualizado:', data);
          loadOrders();
          return;
        }

        if (!data.conversation_id || data.conversation_id !== currentConversationId) {
          console.log("Message filtered out - conversation mismatch:", data.conversation_id, "vs", currentConversationId);
          return;
        }
        {
          const chat = document.getElementById("chat-messages");
          const time = data.timestamp ? new Date(data.timestamp) : new Date();
          const timeStr = time.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
          let msgClass = "message";
          let senderDisplay = data.sender;

          if (data.message_type === "bot" || data.sender === "bot") {
            console.log("Bot message detected:", data);
            msgClass = "message bot";
            senderDisplay = "Bot";
          } else if (data.message_type === "agent" || data.sender === "agent") {
            msgClass = "message agent";
            senderDisplay = "Atendente";
          } else if (data.message_type === "customer" || data.sender === "customer") {
            msgClass = "message";
            senderDisplay = "Cliente";
          } else if (data.sender === "agent") {
            msgClass = "message agent";
            senderDisplay = "Atendente";
          }

          let botBadgeHTML = '';
          if ((data.message_type === "bot" || data.sender === "bot") && data.bot_service) {
            const badgeClass = data.bot_service.toLowerCase();
            botBadgeHTML = `<span class="bot-badge ${badgeClass}">${data.bot_service}</span>`;
          }

          const messageDiv = document.createElement("div");
          messageDiv.className = msgClass;
          const messageContent = data.message || data.content || '';
          messageDiv.innerHTML = `
          <div class="message-content-wrapper">
            <div class="message-header">
              <span class="sender-name">${senderDisplay}</span>
              ${botBadgeHTML}
            </div>
            <div class="message-text">${messageContent}</div>
            <div class="message-footer">
              <span class="message-timestamp">${timeStr}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          `;
          
          chat.appendChild(messageDiv);

          removeMensagensDuplicadas();
          chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;

        }
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        console.error("Erro no WebSocket. Verifique se o token Ã© vÃ¡lido.");
      };
    }

    // NotificaÃ§Ã£o de novo pedido
    function showOrderNotification(order) {
      // Som de notificaÃ§Ã£o
      try {
        const audio = new Audio('data:audio/wav;base64,UklGRnoGAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEAQB8AAEAfAAABAAgAZGF0YQoGAACBhYqFbF1fdH2JkZiYl5eSjImEf35/goeMkZaZmpmYlZGNiYWBf4CGi5CUl5mZmJaSkY2JhYKAg4iNkpWYmZiWk5CL');
        audio.volume = 0.3;
        audio.play().catch(() => {});
      } catch(e) {}

      // NotificaÃ§Ã£o do navegador
      if (Notification.permission === 'granted') {
        new Notification('Novo Pedido!', {
          body: `#${order.id} - ${order.customer_name}\nR$ ${order.total.toFixed(2)}`,
          icon: '/static/icon.png'
        });
      }

      // Toast na tela
      const toast = document.createElement('div');
      toast.style.cssText = `
        position: fixed; top: 20px; right: 20px; z-index: 9999;
        background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea, #764ba2);
        color: white; padding: 15px 25px; border-radius: 10px;
        box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        animation: slideIn 0.3s ease;
      `;
      toast.innerHTML = `
        <strong>Novo Pedido #${order.id}</strong><br>
        ${order.customer_name}<br>
        <small>${order.endereco_entrega}, ${order.bairro_entrega}</small><br>
        <strong>R$ ${order.total.toFixed(2)}</strong>
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(toast);
      setTimeout(() => toast.remove(), 5000);
    }

    // ===== PEDIDOS =====
    function toggleOrders() {
      const panel = document.getElementById('orders-panel');
      panel.classList.toggle('show');
      if (panel.classList.contains('show')) {
        loadOrders();
      }
    }

    async function loadOrders() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/orders/pending', {
          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });
        if (response.ok) {
          const orders = await response.json();
          renderOrders(orders);
          updateOrdersCount(orders.length);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar pedidos:', error);
      }
    }

    function renderOrders(orders) {
      const container = document.getElementById('orders-list');

      if (orders.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Nenhum pedido pendente</p>';
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = orders.map(order => `
        <div class="order-card-mini ${order.status}">
          <h4>
            Pedido #${order.id}
            <span class="order-badge">${order.status.toUpperCase()}</span>
          </h4>
          <p><strong>${order.endereco_entrega}, ${order.numero_entrega}</strong></p>
          <p>${order.bairro_entrega}</p>
          <p style="color: #667eea; font-weight: bold;">R$ ${order.total.toFixed(2)}</p>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    function updateOrdersCount(count) {
      const badge = document.getElementById('orders-count');
      const toggle = document.querySelector('.orders-toggle');
      badge.textContent = count;

      if (count > 0) {
        toggle.classList.add('has-orders');
      } else {
        toggle.classList.remove('has-orders');
      }
    }

    function highlightSelected(conversationId) {
      document.querySelectorAll(".conversation").forEach(div => {
        if (parseInt(div.dataset.id) === conversationId) {
          div.classList.add("active");
        } else {
          div.classList.remove("active");
        }
      });
    }

    async function fetchMyConversations() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/my-conversations", {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        });

        if (res.status === 401) {
          showError("SessÃ£o expirada. FaÃ§a login novamente.");
          localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
          window.location.href = "index.html";
          return;
        }

    

        const myData = await res.json();

        const activeConversations = myData.filter(c => c.status === "pending");
        
        document.getElementById("my-conversations").innerHTML = myData.map(c => `
          <div class="conversation" onclick="loadChat(${c.id})">
            <p><strong>Nome:</strong> ${c.name ?? '(Sem nome)'}</p>
            <p><strong>NÃºmero:</strong> ${c.customer_number}</p>
            <p><strong>Status:</strong> ${c.status}</p>
            <small>Iniciado em: ${new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo" })}</small>
          </div>
        `).join("");
        
        removeMensagensDuplicadas();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao carregar minhas conversas:", error);
      }
    }

    async function fetchAllConversations() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/conversations", {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        });

        if (res.status === 401) {
          showError("SessÃ£o expirada. FaÃ§a login novamente.");
          localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
          window.location.href = "index.html";
          return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        document.getElementById("all-conversations").innerHTML = data
          .filter(c => c.status === "pending")
          .map(c => `
            <div class="conversation" data-id="${c.id}" onclick="loadChat(${c.id})">
              <p><strong>Nome:</strong> ${c.name ?? '(Sem nome)'}</p>
              <p><strong>NÃºmero:</strong> ${c.customer_number}</p>
              <p><strong>Status:</strong> ${c.status}</p>
            </div>
          `).join("");
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao carregar todas as conversas:", error);
      }
    }

    async function loadChat(conversationId) {
      if (currentConversationId !== conversationId) {
        currentConversationId = conversationId;

        // Atribui a conversa
        const assignRes = await fetch(`/conversations/${conversationId}/assign`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`  }
        });

        if (!assignRes.ok) {
          console.error("Erro ao atribuir conversa:", assignRes.status);
          showError("Erro ao atribuir conversa.");
          return;
        }

        // Atualiza lista apÃ³s atribuiÃ§Ã£o
        fetchMyConversations();
        fetchAllConversations();
      }


      highlightSelected(conversationId);

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/conversations/${conversationId}/messages`, {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        });

        const messages = await res.json();
        const chat = document.getElementById("chat-messages");

        chat.innerHTML = messages.map(m => {
          let msgClass = "message";
          let senderDisplay = m.sender;
          let botIndicator = "";

          if (m.message_type === "bot" || m.sender === "bot") {
            msgClass = "message bot";
            senderDisplay = "Bot";
            if (m.bot_service) {
              botIndicator = `<span class="bot-indicator">[${m.bot_service}]</span>`;
            }
          } else if (m.message_type === "agent" || m.sender === "agent") {
            msgClass = "message agent";
            senderDisplay = "Atendente";
          } else if (m.message_type === "customer" || m.sender === "customer") {
            msgClass = "message";
            senderDisplay = "Cliente";
          } else if (m.sender === "agent") {
            msgClass = "message agent";
            senderDisplay = "Atendente";
          }
          
          let botBadgeHTML = '';
          if (m.message_type === "bot" && m.bot_service) {
            const badgeClass = m.bot_service.toLowerCase();
            botBadgeHTML =  `<span class="bot-badge ${badgeClass}">${m.bot_service}</span>`;
          }

          const timeStr = new Date(m.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
          return `
            <div class="${msgClass}">
              <div class="message-content-wrapper">
                <div class="message-header">
                  <span class="sender-name">${senderDisplay}</span>
                  ${botBadgeHTML}
                </div>
                <div class="message-text">${m.content}</div>
                <div class="message-footer">
                  <span class="message-timestamp">${timeStr}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join("");


        removeMensagensDuplicadas();

        chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao carregar mensagens:", error);
      }
    }

    function logout() {
      localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
      window.location.href = "index.html";
    }

    let isSending = false;


    async function sendMessage() {
      if (isSending) return;
      isSending = true;

      console.log("sendMessage Chamada");

      const input = document.getElementById("message-input");
      const content = input.value.trim();

      if (!content || !currentConversationId) {
        console.warn("Mensagem vazia ou conversa nÃ£o selecionada");
        isSending = false;
        return;
      }

      input.disabled = true;
      const sendButton = document.querySelector('button[onclick="sendMessage()"]');
      if (sendButton) sendButton.disabled = true;

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/conversations/${currentConversationId}/reply`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({ message: content })
        });

        if (!res.ok) {
          const errorText = await res.text();
          console.error("Erro na resposta da API:", res.status, await res.text());
          showError("Erro ao enviar mensagem: " + errorText);
          return;
        }

        const chat = document.getElementById("chat-messages");
        const now = new Date();
        const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

        const messageDiv = document.createElement("div");
        messageDiv.className = "message agent";
        messageDiv.innerHTML = `
        <div class="message-content-wrapper">
          <div class="message-header">
            <span class="sender-name">VocÃª</span>
          </div>
          <div class="message-text">${content}</div>
          <div class="message-footer">
            <span class="message-timestamp">${timeStr}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        `
        chat.appendChild(messageDiv);
        
        chat.scrollTop = chat.scrollHeight;
        input.value = "";

        highlightSelected(currentConversationId)
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao enviar mensagem:", error);
        showError("Erro ao enviar mensagem", error);
      } finally {
        isSending = false;
        input.disabled = false;
        if (sendButton) sendButton.disabled = false;
      }
    }


    async function endConversation() {
      console.log("endConversation called", {currentConversationId});
      if (!currentConversationId) {
        showError("Nenhuma conversa selecionada para encerrar.");
        return;
      }

      try {
        console.log("Enviando requisiÃ§Ã£o para encerrar conversa:", currentConversationId);

        const res = await fetch(`/conversations/${currentConversationId}/end`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {Authorization:  `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json"},
        });

        console.log("Resposta recebida:", res.status);

        if (!res.ok) {
          const errorText = await res.text();
          console.error("Erro na resposta da API:", res.status, errorText);
          showError("Erro ao encerrar conversa: " + res.status + " - " + errorText);
          return;
        }

        const result = await res.json();
        console.log("Conversa encerrada com sucesso:", result);

        showToast("Conversa encerrada com sucesso.");
        document.getElementById("chat-messages").innerHTML = "";
        currentConversationId = null;
        fetchMyConversations();
        }  catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao encerrar conversa:", error);
        showError("Erro de conexÃ£o: " + error.message);
      }
    }


    function removeMensagensDuplicadas() {
      const chat = document.getElementById("chat-messages");
      const seen = new Set();
      const messages = Array.from(chat.children);
      const toRemove = [];

      messages.forEach(msg => {
        const senderElem = msg.querySelector(".sender");
        if (!senderElem) return;

        const fullText = senderElem.innerText.trim();

        const messageText = fullText.includes(":") ? fullText.split(":").slice(1).join(":").trim() : fullText;

        const key = messageText;

        if (seen.has(key)) {
          toRemove.push(msg);
        } else {
          seen.add(key);
        }
      });

      toRemove.forEach(msg => msg.remove());
    }


    function formatDate(isoDateStr) {
      try {
        if (!isoDateStr) return "Data InvÃ¡lida";
        const date = new Date(isoDateStr);
        if (isNaN(date)) return "Data InvÃ¡lida";
        return date.toLocaleString("pt-BR", { timeZone: "America/Sao_Paulo" });
      } catch {
        return "Data invÃ¡lida";
      }
    }
    
    async function criarConversaTeste() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/conversations", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            customer_number: "+554196950370",  // Use your real WhatsApp number
            initial_message: "OlÃ¡, isso Ã© um teste automÃ¡tico"
          })
        });

        if (!res.ok) {
          console.error("Erro ao criar conversa de teste:", await res.text());
          return;
        }

        showToast("Conversa teste criada");
        fetchAllConversations();
        fetchMyConversations();
        } catch (error) {
          console.error("Erro ao criar conversa de teste:", error);
        }
      }


      document.getElementById("message-input").addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
        if (e.key === "Enter") {
          e.preventDefault();
          sendMessage();
        }
      });


      function showSuccess(message) {
        Swal.fire({
          icon: 'success',
          title: message,
          showConfirmButton: false,
          timer: 2000
        });
      }

      function showError(message) {
        Swal.fire({
          icon:'error',
          title: 'Erro',
          text: message
        });
      }

      function showToast(message, type="success") {
        Swal.fire({
          toast: true,
          position: 'top-end',
          icon: type,
          title: message,
          showConfirmButton: false,
          timer: 2000,
          timerProgressBar: true
        });
      }

    

    // Add clearFilter function  
    function clearFilter() {
      document.getElementById('conversation-filter').value = 'all';
      fetchAllConversations();
    }

    // InicializaÃ§Ã£o
    connectWebSocket();
    fetchMyConversations();
    loadOrders();
    setInterval(loadOrders, 30000); // Atualiza pedidos a cada 30s
    // Enhanced conversation filtering functions
    async function fetchBotInteractions() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/bot-interactions", {
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
        });

        if (res.status === 401) {
          showError("SessÃ£o expirada. FaÃ§a login novamente.");
          localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
          window.location.href = "index.html";
          return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        document.getElementById("bot-interactions-list").innerHTML = data
          .map(interaction => `
            <div class="conversation">
              <div><strong>${interaction.customer_name}</strong></div>
              <div><small>${interaction.phone_number}</small></div>
              <div>Pergunta: ${interaction.user_message}</div>
              <div style="color: #2e7d32;">Bot: ${interaction.bot_response.substring(0, 100)}...</div>
              <div><small>Bot: ${interaction.bot_type} | ${new Date(interaction.timestamp).toLocaleString()}</small></div>
            </div>
          `).join("");
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro:", error);
        document.getElementById("bot-interactions-list").innerHTML = "<p>Erro ao carregar interaÃ§Ãµes bot</p>";
      }
    }

    async function filterConversations() {
      const filterType = document.getElementById('conversation-filter').value;
      
      if (filterType === 'bot_only') {
        // Show bot interactions section
        document.getElementById('all-conversations').style.display = 'none';
        document.getElementById('bot-interactions').style.display = 'block';
        fetchBotInteractions();
      } else {
        // Show regular conversations with filter
        document.getElementById('all-conversations').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('bot-interactions').style.display = 'none';
        
        try {
          const res = await fetch(`/conversations?filter_type=${filterType}`, {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }
          });

          if (res.status === 401) {
            showError("SessÃ£o expirada. FaÃ§a login novamente.");
            localStorage.removeItem("access_token");
            window.location.href = "index.html";
            return;
          }

          const data = await res.json();
          document.getElementById("all-conversations").innerHTML = data
            .map(c => `
              <div class="conversation" onclick="loadChat(${c.id})">
                <div><strong>${c.name || c.customer_number}</strong></div>
                <div>Status: ${c.status}</div>
                <div><small>Criado: ${new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleString()}</small></div>
              </div>
            `).join("");
        } catch (error) {
          console.error("Erro:", error);
          document.getElementById("all-conversations").innerHTML = "<p>Erro ao carregar conversas</p>";
        }
      }
    }

    fetchAllConversations();
  </script>

</body>
</html>

        <div class="login-card">
            <div class="login-header">
                <h1>Gas Automation</h1>
                <p>Sistema de Automação de Pedidos</p>
            </div>

            <form id="login-form" class="login-form">
                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="username">Usuário</label>
                    <input type="text" id="username" name="username" required>
                </div>

                <div class="form-group">
                    <label for="password">Senha</label>
                    <input type="password" id="password" name="password" required>
                </div>

                <button type="submit" class="btn-primary login-btn">Entrar</button>
            </form>

            <div id="login-message" class="login-message"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="/static/app.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Login functionality
        document.getElementById('login-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();

            const username = document.getElementById('username').value;
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const messageDiv = document.getElementById('login-message');

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/auth/token', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
                    },
                    body: new URLSearchParams({
                        username: username,
                        password: password,
                    }),
                });

                const data = await response.json();

                if (response.ok) {
                    localStorage.setItem('token', data.access_token);
                    window.location.href = '/static/dashboard.html';
                } else {
                    messageDiv.textContent = data.detail || 'Erro no login';
                    messageDiv.className = 'login-message error';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                messageDiv.textContent = 'Erro de conexão';
                messageDiv.className = 'login-message error';
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js"></script>
    <script src="/static/app.js"></script>
    <script>
        let salesChart, ordersStatusChart;

        // Load dashboard data on page load
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadDashboardData();
            initializeCharts();
        });

        async function loadDashboardData() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/dashboard/owner', {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}`
                    }
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    updateMetrics(data.metrics);
                    updateActivity(data.activity);
                    updateTopProducts(data.topProducts);
                    updateCharts(data.chartData);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar dados do dashboard:', error);
            }
        }

        function updateMetrics(metrics) {
            document.getElementById('revenue-today').textContent = `R$ ${metrics.revenueToday.toFixed(2)}`;
            document.getElementById('orders-today').textContent = metrics.ordersToday;
            document.getElementById('new-customers').textContent = metrics.newCustomers;
            document.getElementById('avg-rating').textContent = metrics.avgRating.toFixed(1);

            // Update change indicators
            updateChangeIndicator('revenue-change', metrics.revenueChange);
            updateChangeIndicator('orders-change', metrics.ordersChange);
            updateChangeIndicator('customers-change', metrics.customersChange);
            updateChangeIndicator('rating-change', metrics.ratingChange);
        }

        function updateChangeIndicator(elementId, change) {
            const element = document.getElementById(elementId);
            const isPositive = change >= 0;
            element.textContent = `${isPositive ? '+' : ''}${change.toFixed(1)}%`;
            element.className = `metric-change ${isPositive ? 'positive' : 'negative'}`;
        }

        function updateActivity(activities) {
            const activityList = document.getElementById('recent-activity');
            const html = activities.map(activity => `
                <div class="activity-item">
                    <div class="activity-icon">${getActivityIcon(activity.type)}</div>
                    <div class="activity-content">
                        <div class="activity-text">${activity.description}</div>
                        <div class="activity-time">${formatTimeAgo(activity.timestamp)}</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
            activityList.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateTopProducts(products) {
            const productsList = document.getElementById('top-products');
            const html = products.map(product => `
                <div class="product-item">
                    <div class="product-name">${product.name}</div>
                    <div class="product-sales">${product.sales} vendas</div>
                    <div class="product-revenue">R$ ${product.revenue.toFixed(2)}</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
            productsList.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function initializeCharts() {
            // Sales chart
            const salesCtx = document.getElementById('sales-chart').getContext('2d');
            salesChart = new Chart(salesCtx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: [],
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Vendas (R$)',
                        data: [],
                        borderColor: '#4CAF50',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(76, 175, 80, 0.1)',
                        tension: 0.4
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    scales: {
                        y: {
                            beginAtZero: true,
                            ticks: {
                                callback: function(value) {
                                    return 'R$ ' + value.toFixed(2);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

            // Orders status chart
            const ordersCtx = document.getElementById('orders-status-chart').getContext('2d');
            ordersStatusChart = new Chart(ordersCtx, {
                type: 'doughnut',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Pendente', 'Confirmado', 'Em Trânsito', 'Entregue', 'Cancelado'],
                    datasets: [{
                        data: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0],
                        backgroundColor: [
                            '#FFC107',
                            '#2196F3',
                            '#FF9800',
                            '#4CAF50',
                            '#F44336'
                        ]
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true
                }
            });
        }

        function updateCharts(chartData) {
            if (chartData.sales) {
                salesChart.data.labels = chartData.sales.labels;
                salesChart.data.datasets[0].data = chartData.sales.data;
                salesChart.update();
            }

            if (chartData.ordersStatus) {
                ordersStatusChart.data.datasets[0].data = chartData.ordersStatus;
                ordersStatusChart.update();
            }
        }

        function getActivityIcon(type) {
            const icons = {
                'order': '📦',
                'payment': '💰',
                'customer': '👥',
                'delivery': '🚚',
                'system': '⚙️'
            };
            return icons[type] || '📝';
        }

        function formatTimeAgo(timestamp) {
            const now = new Date();
            const time = new Date(timestamp);
            const diff = now - time;

            const minutes = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
            const hours = Math.floor(diff / 3600000);
            const days = Math.floor(diff / 86400000);

            if (days > 0) return `${days} dia${days > 1 ? 's' : ''} atrás`;
            if (hours > 0) return `${hours} hora${hours > 1 ? 's' : ''} atrás`;
            if (minutes > 0) return `${minutes} minuto${minutes > 1 ? 's' : ''} atrás`;
            return 'Agora';
        }

        function exportReport() {
            // Implement report export functionality
            alert('Funcionalidade de exportação em desenvolvimento');
        }

        function showSettings() {
            document.getElementById('settings-modal').style.display = 'block';
            loadSettings();
        }

        function closeSettings() {
            document.getElementById('settings-modal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        async function loadSettings() {
            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/settings', {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}`
                    }
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    const settings = await response.json();
                    document.getElementById('business-hours').value = settings.businessHours || '';
                    document.getElementById('delivery-fee').value = settings.deliveryFee || '';
                    document.getElementById('min-order').value = settings.minOrder || '';
                    document.getElementById('auto-accept-orders').checked = settings.autoAcceptOrders || false;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar configurações:', error);
            }
        }

        // Settings form submission
        document.getElementById('settings-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();

            const settings = {
                businessHours: document.getElementById('business-hours').value,
                deliveryFee: parseFloat(document.getElementById('delivery-fee').value) || 0,
                minOrder: parseFloat(document.getElementById('min-order').value) || 0,
                autoAcceptOrders: document.getElementById('auto-accept-orders').checked
            };

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/settings', {
                    method: 'PUT',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}`
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(settings)
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    alert('Configurações salvas com sucesso!');
                    closeSettings();
                } else {
                    alert('Erro ao salvar configurações');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                alert('Erro de conexão');
            }
        });

        // Auto refresh every 5 minutes
        setInterval(loadDashboardData, 300000);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="/static/app.js"></script>
    <script>
        let currentPage = 1;
        let totalPages = 1;
        const logsPerPage = 50;

        // Load logs on page load
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadLogs();

            // Set default date range (last 7 days)
            const today = new Date();
            const weekAgo = new Date(today.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

            document.getElementById('date-to').value = today.toISOString().split('T')[0];
            document.getElementById('date-from').value = weekAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0];
        });

        // Refresh button
        document.getElementById('refresh-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            currentPage = 1;
            loadLogs();
        });

        // Filter change events
        document.getElementById('log-level').addEventListener('change', () => {
            currentPage = 1;
            loadLogs();
        });

        document.getElementById('date-from').addEventListener('change', () => {
            currentPage = 1;
            loadLogs();
        });

        document.getElementById('date-to').addEventListener('change', () => {
            currentPage = 1;
            loadLogs();
        });

        document.getElementById('search').addEventListener('input', debounce(() => {
            currentPage = 1;
            loadLogs();
        }, 500));

        // Pagination
        document.getElementById('prev-page').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (currentPage > 1) {
                currentPage--;
                loadLogs();
            }
        });

        document.getElementById('next-page').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (currentPage < totalPages) {
                currentPage++;
                loadLogs();
            }
        });

        async function loadLogs() {
            const loadingEl = document.getElementById('loading');
            const noResultsEl = document.getElementById('no-results');
            const logsBody = document.getElementById('logs-body');

            loadingEl.style.display = 'block';
            noResultsEl.style.display = 'none';

            const filters = {
                level: document.getElementById('log-level').value,
                date_from: document.getElementById('date-from').value,
                date_to: document.getElementById('date-to').value,
                search: document.getElementById('search').value,
                page: currentPage,
                per_page: logsPerPage
            };

            try {
                const queryParams = new URLSearchParams();
                Object.entries(filters).forEach(([key, value]) => {
                    if (value) queryParams.append(key, value);
                });

                const response = await fetch(`/api/logs?${queryParams}`, {
                    headers: {
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}`
                    }
                });

                if (response.ok) {
                    const data = await response.json();
                    displayLogs(data.logs);
                    updatePagination(data.total_pages, data.total_count);
                } else {
                    logsBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="error">Erro ao carregar logs</td></tr>';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                logsBody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="5" class="error">Erro de conexão</td></tr>';
            } finally {
                loadingEl.style.display = 'none';
            }
        }

        function displayLogs(logs) {
            const logsBody = document.getElementById('logs-body');
            const noResultsEl = document.getElementById('no-results');

            if (logs.length === 0) {
                logsBody.innerHTML = '';
                noResultsEl.style.display = 'block';
                return;
            }

            noResultsEl.style.display = 'none';

            const html = logs.map(log => `
                <tr class="log-row log-${log.level.toLowerCase()}">
                    <td>${formatDateTime(log.timestamp)}</td>
                    <td><span class="log-level log-level-${log.level.toLowerCase()}">${log.level}</span></td>
                    <td>${log.module || '-'}</td>
                    <td class="log-message">${escapeHtml(log.message)}</td>
                    <td>
                        ${log.details ? `<button class="btn-small" onclick="showLogDetails(${JSON.stringify(log.details).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')})">Ver</button>` : '-'}
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');

            logsBody.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updatePagination(totalPagesCount, totalCount) {
            totalPages = totalPagesCount;
            const pageInfo = document.getElementById('page-info');
            const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prev-page');
            const nextBtn = document.getElementById('next-page');

            pageInfo.textContent = `Página ${currentPage} de ${totalPages} (${totalCount} logs)`;

            prevBtn.disabled = currentPage <= 1;
            nextBtn.disabled = currentPage >= totalPages;
        }

        function showLogDetails(details) {
            alert(JSON.stringify(details, null, 2));
        }

        function formatDateTime(timestamp) {
            const date = new Date(timestamp);
            return date.toLocaleString('pt-BR');
        }

        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }

        function debounce(func, wait) {
            let timeout;
            return function executedFunction(...args) {
                const later = () => {
                    clearTimeout(timeout);
                    func(...args);
                };
                clearTimeout(timeout);
                timeout = setTimeout(later, wait);
            };
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Configuracao
        const API_URL = '';
        let token = localStorage.getItem('token') || localStorage.getItem('access_token');
        let websocket = null;
        let currentConversation = null;
        let map = null;
        let deliveryMarkers = {};
        let currentTab = 'today';

        // Verificar autenticacao
        if (!token) {
            window.location.href = '/static/index.html';
        }

        // WebSocket
        function connectWebSocket() {
            const wsProtocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
            websocket = new WebSocket(`${wsProtocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/dashboard`);

            websocket.onopen = () => {
                console.log('WebSocket conectado');
                updateConnectionStatus(true);
            };

            websocket.onmessage = (event) => {
                const data = JSON.parse(event.data);
                handleWebSocketMessage(data);
            };

            websocket.onclose = () => {
                console.log('WebSocket desconectado');
                updateConnectionStatus(false);
                setTimeout(connectWebSocket, 3000);
            };

            websocket.onerror = (error) => {
                console.error('WebSocket erro:', error);
                updateConnectionStatus(false);
            };
        }

        function updateConnectionStatus(connected) {
            const statusDot = document.getElementById('ws-status');
            const statusText = document.getElementById('ws-status-text');
            const chatStatus = document.getElementById('chat-ws-status');

            if (connected) {
                statusDot.className = 'status-dot connected';
                chatStatus.className = 'status-dot connected';
                statusText.textContent = 'Conectado';
            } else {
                statusDot.className = 'status-dot disconnected';
                chatStatus.className = 'status-dot disconnected';
                statusText.textContent = 'Desconectado';
            }
        }

        function handleWebSocketMessage(data) {
            console.log('Mensagem recebida:', data);

            if (data.type === 'new_message') {
                if (currentConversation === data.data.phone) {
                    addMessageToChat(data.data);
                }
                loadConversations();
                playNotificationSound();
            }

            if (data.type === 'new_order') {
                showNotification('Novo Pedido!', `#${data.data.order_number || data.data.id}`);
                loadOrders();
                updateStats();
                playNotificationSound();
            }

            if (data.type === 'order_update') {
                loadOrders();
                updateStats();
            }
        }

        // API Functions
        async function fetchAPI(endpoint, options = {}) {
            const defaultOptions = {
                headers: {
                    'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}`,
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                }
            };

            const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}${endpoint}`, {
                ...defaultOptions,
                ...options,
                headers: { ...defaultOptions.headers, ...options.headers }
            });

            if (response.status === 401) {
                localStorage.removeItem('token');
                localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
                window.location.href = '/static/index.html';
                return null;
            }

            return response;
        }

        // Pedidos
        async function loadOrders() {
            try {
                let endpoint = '/api/orders';
                if (currentTab === 'pending') {
                    endpoint = '/api/orders?status=pending';
                } else if (currentTab === 'today') {
                    endpoint = '/api/orders/today';
                }

                const response = await fetchAPI(endpoint);
                if (response && response.ok) {
                    const orders = await response.json();
                    renderOrders(orders);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar pedidos:', error);
            }
        }

        function renderOrders(orders) {
            const container = document.getElementById('orders-container');

            if (!orders || orders.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center; grid-column: 1/-1;">Nenhum pedido encontrado</p>';
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = orders.map(order => `
                <div class="order-card" id="order-${order.id}">
                    <div class="order-header">
                        <span class="order-id">#${order.order_number || order.id}</span>
                        <span class="order-status status-${order.status}">${formatStatus(order.status)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="order-info">
                        <p><strong>Bairro:</strong> ${order.delivery_bairro || 'N/A'}</p>
                        <p><strong>Total:</strong> R$ ${parseFloat(order.total_amount || 0).toFixed(2)}</p>
                        <p><strong>Pagamento:</strong> ${order.payment_method || 'N/A'}</p>
                        <p><small>Criado: ${formatDate(order.created_at)}</small></p>
                    </div>
                    <div class="order-actions">
                        ${getOrderActions(order)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }

        function getOrderActions(order) {
            const actions = [];

            if (order.status === 'pending') {
                actions.push(`<button class="btn btn-success" onclick="updateOrderStatus('${order.id}', 'paid')">Confirmar Pago</button>`);
                actions.push(`<button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="cancelOrder('${order.id}')">Cancelar</button>`);
            }

            if (order.status === 'paid') {
                actions.push(`<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="updateOrderStatus('${order.id}', 'preparing')">Preparando</button>`);
            }

            if (order.status === 'preparing') {
                actions.push(`<button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="updateOrderStatus('${order.id}', 'dispatched')">Saiu p/ Entrega</button>`);
            }

            if (order.status === 'dispatched') {
                actions.push(`<button class="btn btn-success" onclick="updateOrderStatus('${order.id}', 'delivered')">Entregue</button>`);
            }

            return actions.join('');
        }

        async function updateOrderStatus(orderId, status) {
            try {
                const response = await fetchAPI(`/api/orders/${orderId}/status`, {
                    method: 'PATCH',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ status })
                });

                if (response && response.ok) {
                    showToast('Status atualizado!', 'success');
                    loadOrders();
                    updateStats();
                } else {
                    showToast('Erro ao atualizar status', 'error');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro:', error);
                showToast('Erro ao atualizar status', 'error');
            }
        }

        async function cancelOrder(orderId) {
            const result = await Swal.fire({
                title: 'Cancelar Pedido?',
                text: 'Esta acao nao pode ser desfeita',
                icon: 'warning',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#d33',
                cancelButtonColor: '#3085d6',
                confirmButtonText: 'Sim, cancelar',
                cancelButtonText: 'Nao'
            });

            if (result.isConfirmed) {
                await updateOrderStatus(orderId, 'cancelled');
            }
        }

        // Stats
        async function updateStats() {
            try {
                const response = await fetchAPI('/api/orders/today');
                if (response && response.ok) {
                    const orders = await response.json();

                    const total = orders.length;
                    const delivered = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'delivered').length;
                    const revenue = orders
                        .filter(o => o.status !== 'cancelled')
                        .reduce((sum, o) => sum + parseFloat(o.total_amount || 0), 0);

                    document.getElementById('stat-pedidos').textContent = total;
                    document.getElementById('stat-entregas').textContent = delivered;
                    document.getElementById('stat-faturamento').textContent = `R$ ${revenue.toFixed(2)}`;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao atualizar stats:', error);
            }
        }

        // Conversas
        async function loadConversations() {
            try {
                const response = await fetchAPI('/api/chats');
                if (response && response.ok) {
                    const conversations = await response.json();
                    renderConversations(conversations);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar conversas:', error);
            }
        }

        function renderConversations(conversations) {
            const container = document.getElementById('conversation-list');

            if (!conversations || conversations.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center; padding: 20px;">Nenhuma conversa</p>';
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = conversations.map(conv => `
                <div class="conversation-item ${currentConversation === conv.phone ? 'active' : ''}"
                     onclick="selectConversation('${conv.phone}')">
                    <div class="conversation-name">
                        ${conv.customer_name || formatPhone(conv.phone)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="conversation-preview">${conv.last_message || 'Sem mensagens'}</div>
                </div>
            `).join('');
        }

        async function selectConversation(phone) {
            currentConversation = phone;
            document.getElementById('message-input').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('send-btn').disabled = false;

            loadConversations();

            try {
                const response = await fetchAPI(`/api/chats/${phone}/messages`);
                if (response && response.ok) {
                    const messages = await response.json();
                    renderMessages(messages);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao carregar mensagens:', error);
            }
        }

        function renderMessages(messages) {
            const container = document.getElementById('chat-messages');

            if (!messages || messages.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p style="color: #999; text-align: center;">Sem mensagens</p>';
                return;
            }

            container.innerHTML = messages.map(msg => {
                const isOutgoing = msg.from_me;
                return `
                    <div class="message ${isOutgoing ? 'outgoing' : 'incoming'}">
                        <div class="message-bubble">${escapeHtml(msg.text)}</div>
                        <div class="message-info">${formatTime(msg.timestamp)}</div>
                    </div>
                `;
            }).join('');

            container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
        }

        function addMessageToChat(data) {
            const container = document.getElementById('chat-messages');
            const isOutgoing = data.direction === 'outgoing';

            const messageDiv = document.createElement('div');
            messageDiv.className = `message ${isOutgoing ? 'outgoing' : 'incoming'}`;
            messageDiv.innerHTML = `
                <div class="message-bubble">${escapeHtml(data.message)}</div>
                <div class="message-info">${formatTime(new Date())}</div>
            `;

            container.appendChild(messageDiv);
            container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
        }

        async function sendMessage() {
            const input = document.getElementById('message-input');
            const message = input.value.trim();

            if (!message || !currentConversation) return;

            try {
                const response = await fetchAPI(`/api/chats/${currentConversation}/send`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    body: JSON.stringify({ phone: currentConversation, message })
                });

                if (response && response.ok) {
                    input.value = '';
                    addMessageToChat({ message, direction: 'outgoing' });
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Erro ao enviar mensagem:', error);
            }
        }

        function handleKeyPress(event) {
            if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                sendMessage();
            }
        }

        // Mapa
        function initMap() {
            // Coordenadas de Curitiba
            map = L.map('map').setView([-25.4284, -49.2733], 12);

            L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
                attribution: '&copy; OpenStreetMap contributors'
            }).addTo(map);

            // Adicionar marcador da loja
            L.marker([-25.4284, -49.2733])
                .addTo(map)
                .bindPopup('<b>Gas Automation</b><br>Sede');
        }

        function updateDeliveryMarker(deliveryId, lat, lng, info) {
            if (deliveryMarkers[deliveryId]) {
                deliveryMarkers[deliveryId].setLatLng([lat, lng]);
            } else {
                const marker = L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(map);
                marker.bindPopup(`<b>Entrega #${deliveryId}</b><br>${info}`);
                deliveryMarkers[deliveryId] = marker;
            }
        }

        // UI Functions
        function showTab(tab) {
            currentTab = tab;
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {
                btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(tab));
            });
            loadOrders();
        }

        function showSection(section) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.menu-item').forEach(item => item.classList.remove('active'));
            event.target.classList.add('active');

            // Implementar navegacao entre secoes se necessario
            if (section === 'pedidos') {
                loadOrders();
            }
        }

        // Utilidades
        function formatStatus(status) {
            const statusMap = {
                'pending': 'Pendente',
                'paid': 'Pago',
                'preparing': 'Preparando',
                'dispatched': 'Em Entrega',
                'delivered': 'Entregue',
                'cancelled': 'Cancelado'
            };
            return statusMap[status] || status;
        }

        function formatPhone(phone) {
            if (!phone) return '';
            const cleaned = phone.replace(/\D/g, '');
            if (cleaned.length === 13) {
                return `+${cleaned.slice(0,2)} (${cleaned.slice(2,4)}) ${cleaned.slice(4,9)}-${cleaned.slice(9)}`;
            }
            return phone;
        }

        function formatDate(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return '';
            return new Date(dateStr).toLocaleString('pt-BR');
        }

        function formatTime(timestamp) {
            if (!timestamp) return '';
            return new Date(timestamp).toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
        }

        function escapeHtml(text) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.textContent = text;
            return div.innerHTML;
        }

        function playNotificationSound() {
            // Som de notificacao
        }

        function showNotification(title, body) {
            if (Notification.permission === 'granted') {
                new Notification(title, { body });
            }
        }

        function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
            Swal.fire({
                toast: true,
                position: 'top-end',
                icon: type,
                title: message,
                showConfirmButton: false,
                timer: 3000
            });
        }

        function logout() {
            localStorage.removeItem('token');
            localStorage.removeItem('access_token');
            window.location.href = '/static/index.html';
        }

        // Inicializacao
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            if (Notification.permission === 'default') {
                Notification.requestPermission();
            }

            connectWebSocket();
            initMap();
            loadConversations();
            loadOrders();
            updateStats();

            setInterval(loadOrders, 30000);
            setInterval(updateStats, 60000);
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="/static/app.js"></script>
    <script>
        // Form submission
        document.getElementById('customer-form').addEventListener('submit', async (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();

            const formData = new FormData(e.target);
            const customerData = {
                name: formData.get('name'),
                cpf: formData.get('cpf'),
                phone: formData.get('phone'),
                email: formData.get('email'),
                address: formData.get('address'),
                neighborhood: formData.get('neighborhood'),
                city: formData.get('city'),
                cep: formData.get('cep'),
                complement: formData.get('complement'),
                notes: formData.get('notes'),
                active: formData.get('active') === 'on'
            };

            try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/customers', {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('token')}`
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(customerData),
                });

                const result = await response.json();

                if (response.ok) {
                    showMessage('Cliente cadastrado com sucesso!', 'success');
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.location.href = '/static/dashboard.html';
                    }, 2000);
                } else {
                    showMessage(result.detail || 'Erro ao cadastrar cliente', 'error');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                showMessage('Erro de conexão. Tente novamente.', 'error');
            }
        });

        // Cancel button
        document.getElementById('cancel-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            window.location.href = '/static/dashboard.html';
        });

        // CPF formatting
        document.getElementById('cpf').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            let value = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
            if (value.length <= 11) {
                value = value.replace(/(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{3})(\d{2})/, '$1.$2.$3-$4');
                e.target.value = value;
            }
        });

        // Phone formatting
        document.getElementById('phone').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            let value = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
            if (value.length <= 11) {
                if (value.length <= 10) {
                    value = value.replace(/(\d{2})(\d{4})(\d{4})/, '($1) $2-$3');
                } else {
                    value = value.replace(/(\d{2})(\d{5})(\d{4})/, '($1) $2-$3');
                }
                e.target.value = value;
            }
        });

        // CEP formatting
        document.getElementById('cep').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            let value = e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '');
            if (value.length <= 8) {
                value = value.replace(/(\d{5})(\d{3})/, '$1-$2');
                e.target.value = value;
            }
        });

        function showMessage(text, type) {
            const messageEl = document.getElementById('message');
            messageEl.textContent = text;
            messageEl.className = `message ${type}`;
            messageEl.style.display = 'block';

            setTimeout(() => {
                messageEl.style.display = 'none';
            }, 5000);
        }
    </script>
